## 核对旧碗真伪后决定暂不购买 · p08-a-01

主题：消费与服务；情景：核对二手碗真伪与损伤（secondhand-bowl-authenticity-check）。

沟通目标：了解卖方无法保证年代及现有磨损情况，决定先查看来源资料而不购买

背景：顾客在二手商店询问一个被标为老物件的陶碗。

人物：Celia、Owen。

起草：author-phase08-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-a-01-s01 · Celia: Is this a genuine antique, or a modern copy?
- p08-a-01-s02 · Owen: I don't know its age. It came from a house clearance, without any documents.
- p08-a-01-s03 · Celia: Can you give me a guarantee that it's at least a hundred years old?
- p08-a-01-s04 · Owen: No, I can't. There's also a chip under the base. I can show you that, but I can't rule out hidden damage.
- p08-a-01-s05 · Celia: I appreciate your honesty. I like it, but the age matters because it's for a collector.
- p08-a-01-s06 · Owen: The owner may still have the purchase receipt. I can ask.
- p08-a-01-s07 · Celia: Please do. I'll leave it for now and come back if there's more information.

目标用法：

- genuine · adj. · 真正的而非仿制的；义项 sense-staged-ox-532f7b98b579d9de-p08-a-01；来源 ox-532f7b98b579d9de；证据 p08-a-01-s01；词形 genuine。
- guarantee · n. · 明确的保证；义项 sense-staged-ox-334063747279a7ed-p08-a-01；来源 ox-334063747279a7ed；证据 p08-a-01-s03；词形 guarantee。
- hidden · adj. · 不易察觉的；隐藏的；义项 sense-staged-ox-c54e33de8b117b82-p08-a-01；来源 ox-c54e33de8b117b82；证据 p08-a-01-s04；词形 hidden。
- honesty · n. · 诚实坦率；义项 sense-staged-ox-09d62ce2cc890905-p08-a-01；来源 ox-09d62ce2cc890905；证据 p08-a-01-s05；词形 honesty。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-a；整篇结论：accepted。

After rereading the complete version 2, the revised hidden sense correctly describes damage that may not be visible, not deliberate concealment. The candid seller cannot establish age, discloses a chip and offers to ask for a receipt; Celia declines purchase for now. The authenticity inquiry, conditional next step and Chinese outcome remain coherent.

绑定文件 SHA-256：3e57b606c043ad28aaa3fd43dc34eaa613ae6988094419b17a8c8cfe2e24faee

- ox-532f7b98b579d9de：accepted；In s01 genuine is the attributive adjective contrasting an authentic antique with a modern copy; the seller's lack of provenance leaves that distinction unresolved.（p08-a-01-s01）。
- ox-334063747279a7ed：accepted；In s03 a guarantee is the requested explicit assurance about being at least a hundred years old; its noun role is shown by the article and give me construction.（p08-a-01-s03）。
- ox-c54e33de8b117b82：accepted；In s04 hidden modifies damage that inspection may not reveal. The revised 不易察觉的；隐藏的 matches this physical uncertainty, and can't rule out does not imply the seller is concealing known defects.（p08-a-01-s04）。
- ox-09d62ce2cc890905：accepted；In s05 honesty is the uncountable quality Celia appreciates after Owen admits uncertainty and points out damage, so 诚实坦率 is grounded in the preceding exchange.（p08-a-01-s05）。

<a id="dialogue-p08-a-02"></a>

## 放弃隆重补偿并约好短暂见面 · p08-a-02

主题：家庭与人际；情景：道歉后约定简单见面（friend-apology-small-next-step）。

沟通目标：承认失约造成的影响，尊重朋友不需要隆重道歉安排的意愿并约好周六喝咖啡

背景：朋友因未提前告知就取消原先的见面安排而道歉。

人物：Nadia、Luis。

起草：author-phase08-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-a-02-s01 · Nadia: I'm genuinely sorry I cancelled without telling you. You waited for me, and that wasn't fair.
- p08-a-02-s02 · Luis: I thought something had happened to you. Then I felt rather foolish.
- p08-a-02-s03 · Nadia: I was going to book a big dinner as a gesture of apology.
- p08-a-02-s04 · Luis: I don't need anything grand. I'd rather know you'll send a message next time.
- p08-a-02-s05 · Nadia: I will. And I won't insist on dinner if that's not what you want.
- p08-a-02-s06 · Luis: A coffee on Saturday would be nice. Just half an hour, though.
- p08-a-02-s07 · Nadia: Saturday at ten, then. Don't hesitate to tell me if you need to change it.
- p08-a-02-s08 · Luis: Ten works. Hopefully, we can put this behind us.
- p08-a-02-s09 · Nadia: Thanks. I'll meet you outside the station café.

目标用法：

- genuinely · adv. · 真诚地；义项 sense-staged-ox-bcb27a23b0177e96-p08-a-02；来源 ox-bcb27a23b0177e96；证据 p08-a-02-s01；词形 genuinely。
- gesture · n. · 表达歉意的举动；义项 sense-staged-ox-c6bff428871859f8-p08-a-02；来源 ox-c6bff428871859f8；证据 p08-a-02-s03；词形 gesture。
- grand · adj. · 隆重铺张的；义项 sense-staged-ox-ed04b4cee5168ee4-p08-a-02；来源 ox-ed04b4cee5168ee4；证据 p08-a-02-s04；词形 grand。
- insist · v. · 坚持要求；义项 sense-staged-ox-b0d845b540861cd3-p08-a-02；来源 ox-b0d845b540861cd3；证据 p08-a-02-s05；词形 insist。
- hesitate · v. · 犹豫而不愿做某事；义项 sense-staged-ox-ff968e3d94b8e00c-p08-a-02；来源 ox-ff968e3d94b8e00c；证据 p08-a-02-s07；词形 hesitate。
- hopefully · adv. · 希望事情能如此；义项 sense-staged-ox-31b08e0e6fe40c29-p08-a-02；来源 ox-31b08e0e6fe40c29；证据 p08-a-02-s08；词形 hopefully。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-a；整篇结论：accepted。

Complete version 2 reread confirms the revised goal describes an apology arrangement rather than a celebration. Nadia acknowledges the missed meeting, accepts Luis's refusal of an elaborate dinner, promises future communication and fixes Saturday at ten outside the station café. The concrete reconciliation plan supports both the title and all six usages.

绑定文件 SHA-256：bff1a918dad31755a9ce9684fa869a94fef7cfab235f7a3c30c5a98f0702b54b

- ox-bcb27a23b0177e96：accepted；In s01 genuinely modifies sorry and marks sincere remorse, with the admission that Luis was left waiting giving the apology substance.（p08-a-02-s01）。
- ox-c6bff428871859f8：accepted；In s03 gesture is the count noun in a gesture of apology, naming the proposed dinner as a symbolic act of making amends.（p08-a-02-s03）。
- ox-ed04b4cee5168ee4：accepted；In s04 grand describes the elaborate proposed dinner, which Luis contrasts with simply being kept informed; 隆重铺张的 fits that contrast.（p08-a-02-s04）。
- ox-b0d845b540861cd3：accepted；In s05 insist on dinner means persist in demanding that arrangement; the negative verb promise respects Luis's expressed preference.（p08-a-02-s05）。
- ox-ff968e3d94b8e00c：accepted；In s07 don't hesitate to tell me invites Luis not to hold back if the coffee appointment must change, with to tell supplying the contemplated action.（p08-a-02-s07）。
- ox-31b08e0e6fe40c29：accepted；In s08 Hopefully expresses the speaker's hope for reconciliation across the whole clause, rather than describing a hopeful manner of performing an action.（p08-a-02-s08）。

## 为小场地演出安排中途休息 · p08-a-03

主题：文娱与运动；情景：安排乐队演出的中场休息（small-venue-band-break-plan）。

沟通目标：依据演出时长与演奏强度确定休息长度和恢复演出的提示

背景：乐队成员在小酒馆演出前和场地经理核对安排。

人物：Maya、Ben。

起草：author-phase08-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-a-03-s01 · Maya: How long is our gig on Friday? The email just says eight until ten.
- p08-a-03-s02 · Ben: That includes an interval. You don't have to play for two hours without a break.
- p08-a-03-s03 · Maya: Good. The last section is quite intense, and our drummer needs time to recover.
- p08-a-03-s04 · Ben: Would a fifteen-minute break after the first set work?
- p08-a-03-s05 · Maya: Yes. We'll finish that set at eight forty-five.
- p08-a-03-s06 · Ben: Then I'll announce that you're back at nine and lower the background music when you're ready.
- p08-a-03-s07 · Maya: Perfect. I'll give you a nod from the stage before we start again.

目标用法：

- gig · n. · 流行乐队的现场演出；义项 sense-staged-ox-3833034541665fe9-p08-a-03；来源 ox-3833034541665fe9；证据 p08-a-03-s01；词形 gig。
- interval · n. · 演出中场休息；义项 sense-staged-ox-92a13e09560df671-p08-a-03；来源 ox-92a13e09560df671；证据 p08-a-03-s02；词形 interval。
- intense · adj. · 紧张激烈的；义项 sense-staged-ox-2827343bbbf67be2-p08-a-03；来源 ox-2827343bbbf67be2；证据 p08-a-03-s03；词形 intense。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The musicians resolve a two-hour booking into a first set ending at 8:45, a fifteen-minute interval, and a 9:00 restart coordinated by a nod and music change. The demanding final section motivates a rest; the Chinese goal accurately describes both duration and restart cue.

绑定文件 SHA-256：5aedce5abb16bb653671188d6d0c9249265e3a6d815128229c9b38ede227c93c

- ox-3833034541665fe9：accepted；In s01 our gig denotes the band's live Friday performance at a small venue, with its duration clarified in the response.（p08-a-03-s01）。
- ox-92a13e09560df671：accepted；In s02 an interval is a break within the performance, explicitly contrasted with two hours of uninterrupted playing and later fixed at fifteen minutes.（p08-a-03-s02）。
- ox-2827343bbbf67be2：accepted；In s03 intense modifies the demanding musical section and is grounded in the drummer's need to recover; 紧张激烈的 describes its energy.（p08-a-03-s03）。

<a id="dialogue-p08-a-04"></a>

## 缩小贸易课程项目的范围并区分解释因素 · p08-a-04

主题：学习与技能；情景：限定贸易项目的证据范围（trade-project-evidence-scope）。

沟通目标：选定两座城市的货运案例，分开核对基础设施和物价资料而不把变化全部归因于全球化

背景：两名学生为经济地理课程缩小研究项目的范围。

人物：Priya、Dan。

起草：author-phase08-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-a-04-s01 · Priya: Our title is 'How globalization changed the world'. That's far too much for ten minutes.
- p08-a-04-s02 · Dan: Could we follow one product as it's shipped around the globe instead?
- p08-a-04-s03 · Priya: Yes. We have records from two industrial cities that make bicycle parts.
- p08-a-04-s04 · Dan: The report says deliveries became increasingly reliable after the new railway opened.
- p08-a-04-s05 · Priya: Then transport infrastructure should be one part of our explanation, not just international trade.
- p08-a-04-s06 · Dan: What about the higher prices? Shall we say they prove the trade links were a mistake?
- p08-a-04-s07 · Priya: No. We haven't separated the effect of inflation from changes in shipping costs.
- p08-a-04-s08 · Dan: I'll check those price figures. You can trace the route and the railway dates.
- p08-a-04-s09 · Priya: Agreed. We'll compare these two cities and make the limits clear.

目标用法：

- globalization · n. · 经济活动跨国联系日益增强的过程；义项 sense-staged-ox-8a46e4219174b1bd-p08-a-04；来源 ox-8a46e4219174b1bd；证据 p08-a-04-s01；词形 globalization。
- globe · n. · 整个世界；义项 sense-staged-ox-337a47a7056d91a3-p08-a-04；来源 ox-337a47a7056d91a3；证据 p08-a-04-s02；词形 globe。
- industrial · adj. · 以工业生产为主的；义项 sense-staged-ox-aa34cc919b983a40-p08-a-04；来源 ox-aa34cc919b983a40；证据 p08-a-04-s03；词形 industrial。
- increasingly · adv. · 越来越；义项 sense-staged-ox-21cb0b941114c0cd-p08-a-04；来源 ox-21cb0b941114c0cd；证据 p08-a-04-s04；词形 increasingly。
- infrastructure · n. · 交通等基础设施；义项 sense-staged-ox-ba5a69f7aabf1086-p08-a-04；来源 ox-ba5a69f7aabf1086；证据 p08-a-04-s05；词形 infrastructure。
- inflation · n. · 整体物价上涨；义项 sense-staged-ox-c6ea674ab289e789-p08-a-04；来源 ox-c6ea674ab289e789；证据 p08-a-04-s07；词形 inflation。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The students narrow an overlarge worldwide topic to two bicycle-part manufacturing cities, distinguish railway infrastructure from trade, and avoid attributing price increases before checking inflation. Their division of research and restricted comparison achieve the Chinese scope goal without unsupported real-world claims.

- ox-8a46e4219174b1bd：accepted；In s01 globalization names the cross-border economic integration proposed as the project's subject; later international trade and shipping make the intended process concrete.（p08-a-04-s01）。
- ox-337a47a7056d91a3：accepted；In s02 around the globe is the idiomatic noun phrase for worldwide shipping, not a physical model of Earth.（p08-a-04-s02）。
- ox-aa34cc919b983a40：accepted；In s03 industrial modifies cities specifically identified as manufacturers of bicycle parts, supporting the production-oriented sense.（p08-a-04-s03）。
- ox-21cb0b941114c0cd：accepted；In s04 increasingly modifies reliable and marks growing reliability over time following the railway opening within the cited fictional report.（p08-a-04-s04）。

## 继续记录温室香草的生长变化 · p08-a-05

主题：自然与环境；情景：安排温室香草的观察记录（greenhouse-herb-observation-plan）。

沟通目标：区分初步观察与确定结论，约定在同一时间拍摄并继续记录香草生长

背景：社区花园的两名志愿者查看新种下的罗勒。

人物：Mei、Arthur。

起草：author-phase08-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-a-05-s01 · Mei: The basil in the greenhouse looks taller than it did last week.
- p08-a-05-s02 · Arthur: It's the first herb I've tried growing, so I keep checking it.
- p08-a-05-s03 · Mei: Our initial measurements are in this notebook. These plants were six centimetres tall.
- p08-a-05-s04 · Arthur: They seem to be growing gradually, rather than shooting up overnight.
- p08-a-05-s05 · Mei: Let's measure them again before we decide how much they've grown. The photo angle can be misleading.
- p08-a-05-s06 · Arthur: I'll take a picture from this same spot every Sunday morning.
- p08-a-05-s07 · Mei: And I'll write down the height beside each date. We can compare them next month.

目标用法：

- greenhouse · n. · 温室；义项 sense-staged-ox-32ef9d39c862fab0-p08-a-05；来源 ox-32ef9d39c862fab0；证据 p08-a-05-s01；词形 greenhouse。
- herb · n. · 供调味等用途的香草植物；义项 sense-staged-ox-e09683124960d651-p08-a-05；来源 ox-e09683124960d651；证据 p08-a-05-s02；词形 herb。
- initial · adj. · 最初的；义项 sense-staged-ox-4c70f62cc613ce5c-p08-a-05；来源 ox-4c70f62cc613ce5c；证据 p08-a-05-s03；词形 initial。
- gradually · adv. · 逐渐地；义项 sense-staged-ox-12fd37d03997e255-p08-a-05；来源 ox-12fd37d03997e255；证据 p08-a-05-s04；词形 gradually。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The volunteers distinguish apparent basil growth from measured change, acknowledge misleading camera angles and agree to weekly same-position photographs plus dated heights. This achieves the Chinese recording plan and makes no horticultural guarantee.

## 依据虚构案例拟定听证提问 · p08-a-06

主题：学习与技能；情景：准备虚构州长听证会的问题（fictional-governor-hearing-question）。

沟通目标：根据课堂材料区分治理责任与单独定价权限，并写出询问收费决定依据的问题

背景：学生讨论公民教育课提供的虚构州政府案例，不涉及现实地区的法律。

人物：Amina、Theo。

起草：author-phase08-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-a-06-s01 · Amina: In this case study, the governor wants to charge a fee for using the park.
- p08-a-06-s02 · Theo: Does being elected to govern the state mean she can set any fee she likes?
- p08-a-06-s03 · Amina: No. The case notes say the assembly must approve new charges.
- p08-a-06-s04 · Theo: Then our question at the mock hearing should be about that approval.
- p08-a-06-s05 · Amina: Yes. Ask whether she intends to impose the fee before the vote.
- p08-a-06-s06 · Theo: Should we describe our inquiry as an accusation?
- p08-a-06-s07 · Amina: Not yet. We only know she proposed it, not that anyone has been charged.
- p08-a-06-s08 · Theo: I'll write, 'What approval would be needed before visitors had to pay?'
- p08-a-06-s09 · Amina: That asks for the missing information without assuming she's broken the rules.

目标用法：

- governor · n. · 州长；义项 sense-staged-ox-2c10aa9664e4e5e6-p08-a-06；来源 ox-2c10aa9664e4e5e6；证据 p08-a-06-s01；词形 governor。
- govern · v. · 治理州或国家；义项 sense-staged-ox-ddfcf67c12e302ac-p08-a-06；来源 ox-ddfcf67c12e302ac；证据 p08-a-06-s02；词形 govern。
- hearing · n. · 听取意见或证据的正式听证会；义项 sense-staged-ox-3b01fbbdf13e3542-p08-a-06；来源 ox-3b01fbbdf13e3542；证据 p08-a-06-s04；词形 hearing。
- impose · v. · 强制实行收费等措施；义项 sense-staged-ox-21b1b3ce05e6f51f-p08-a-06；来源 ox-21b1b3ce05e6f51f；证据 p08-a-06-s05；词形 impose。
- inquiry · n. · 为查明情况而作的询问；义项 sense-staged-ox-4bb6ea4aad1cafe5-p08-a-06；来源 ox-4bb6ea4aad1cafe5；证据 p08-a-06-s06；词形 inquiry。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The classroom's explicitly fictional case supplies assembly approval as a local rule; students distinguish proposing a fee from collecting it and write a neutral question about needed approval. The mock hearing and ending fulfill the Chinese goal without pretending to state real jurisdictional law.

## 分享野餐点心并装好剩余部分 · p08-a-07

主题：饮食沟通；情景：分配野餐点心（picnic-pastry-sharing）。

沟通目标：品尝点心并按食量分配，约定把剩余部分带走

背景：朋友在公园摊开各自带来的野餐食物。

人物：Rosa、Eli。

起草：author-phase08-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-a-07-s01 · Rosa: Those pastries look gorgeous. Did you make them?
- p08-a-07-s02 · Eli: Yes. I took them out when the tops were golden, so they should still be soft inside.
- p08-a-07-s03 · Rosa: My goodness, you brought enough for a party!
- p08-a-07-s04 · Eli: I got carried away. Grab a plate before I open the honey; the lid is sticky.
- p08-a-07-s05 · Rosa: Here you go. I'll have one now. That should take the edge off my hunger.
- p08-a-07-s06 · Eli: Would you like a little honey on it?
- p08-a-07-s07 · Rosa: Just a little, thanks. Let's put the rest in your box rather than leave them out.
- p08-a-07-s08 · Eli: Good idea. We can each take a few home.

- gorgeous · adj. · 非常诱人或漂亮的；义项 sense-staged-ox-5ed93af0763f9d13-p08-a-07；来源 ox-5ed93af0763f9d13；证据 p08-a-07-s01；词形 gorgeous。
- golden · adj. · 金黄色的；义项 sense-staged-ox-87d8301472cf1baf-p08-a-07；来源 ox-87d8301472cf1baf；证据 p08-a-07-s02；词形 golden。
- goodness · n. · 用于表示惊讶的感叹语；义项 sense-staged-ox-7bf7e3c37ed8e383-p08-a-07；来源 ox-7bf7e3c37ed8e383；证据 p08-a-07-s03；词形 goodness。
- grab · v. · 迅速拿取；义项 sense-staged-ox-f577ac9b6b2568df-p08-a-07；来源 ox-f577ac9b6b2568df；证据 p08-a-07-s04；词形 grab。
- honey · n. · 蜂蜜；义项 sense-staged-ox-9416f15384766afe-p08-a-07；来源 ox-9416f15384766afe；证据 p08-a-07-s04；词形 honey。
- hunger · n. · 饥饿感；义项 sense-staged-ox-6a3ff2c7c0aac899-p08-a-07；来源 ox-6a3ff2c7c0aac899；证据 p08-a-07-s05；词形 hunger。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The picnic conversation naturally moves from admiring the pastries to surprise at the quantity, sharing one with honey and agreeing to pack the rest for both friends to take home. The title summarizes their agreed packing action; the goal concerns portion choice and leftovers, not a recipe or safety claim.

## 按初次使用者需要修改传单图文 · p08-a-08

主题：工作与协作；情景：修订操作传单的图文说明（illustrated-leaflet-reader-revision）。

沟通目标：按初次使用者需求补充逐步图示并纳入反馈，约定印刷前测试

背景：设计师和项目负责人检查共享工具柜的操作传单。

人物：Sofia、Marcus。

起草：author-phase08-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-a-08-s01 · Sofia: The graphic designer sent two versions. Which one is clearer to you?
- p08-a-08-s02 · Marcus: The one on the left. The graphics are simpler, but I still can't tell which button opens the drawer.
- p08-a-08-s03 · Sofia: I'll ask her to illustrate the three steps separately.
- p08-a-08-s04 · Marcus: Good. One large illustration of the whole cabinet doesn't show the sequence.
- p08-a-08-s05 · Sofia: The intended audience is people using it for the first time, so we shouldn't assume they know the controls.
- p08-a-08-s06 · Marcus: Can we incorporate the volunteers' comments as well? They keep being asked where to return things.
- p08-a-08-s07 · Sofia: Yes. I'll add a return step and remove the paragraph about our history.
- p08-a-08-s08 · Marcus: Let's try the new version with two first-time users before we print it.

- graphic · adj. · 与图像设计有关的；义项 sense-staged-ox-eb440f72ef2ed320-p08-a-08；来源 ox-eb440f72ef2ed320；证据 p08-a-08-s01；词形 graphic。

- illustrate · v. · 用图示解释；义项 sense-staged-ox-2f4045a7346d28bd-p08-a-08；来源 ox-2f4045a7346d28bd；证据 p08-a-08-s03；词形 illustrate。
- illustration · n. · 说明用的图画；义项 sense-staged-ox-cfd0a8195319d1d5-p08-a-08；来源 ox-cfd0a8195319d1d5；证据 p08-a-08-s04；词形 illustration。
- intended · adj. · 预期面向的；义项 sense-staged-ox-41cb54f79e163953-p08-a-08；来源 ox-41cb54f79e163953；证据 p08-a-08-s05；词形 intended。
- incorporate · v. · 纳入并结合；义项 sense-staged-ox-5e2bef3940fd2960-p08-a-08；来源 ox-5e2bef3940fd2960；证据 p08-a-08-s06；词形 incorporate。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The designer's two versions prompt specific usability criticism: unclear drawer controls, missing sequence and missing return instructions. The speakers commit to separate step images, incorporate volunteer feedback, remove irrelevant history and test with new users before printing, matching the Chinese revision goal.

## 商定维修项目投资会谈的准备分工 · p08-a-10

主题：工作与协作；情景：澄清维修项目的创新与投资问题（repair-startup-investor-questions）。

沟通目标：区分可展示的创新与尚缺比较证据的折扣效果，分工准备成本、样品和现有评论，并暂不承诺投资回报

- p08-a-10-s01 · Jules: An investor has agreed to hear about our repair service next week.
- p08-a-10-s02 · Farah: How much investment are we asking for, and what would it pay for?
- p08-a-10-s03 · Jules: Enough for a second workbench and a booking system. The figures are still rough.
- p08-a-10-s04 · Farah: Then let's get those costs right before calling the whole business innovative.
- p08-a-10-s05 · Jules: The main innovation is our reusable replacement panel. Customers don't have to replace the whole case.
- p08-a-10-s06 · Farah: That's something we can demonstrate. What evidence do we have that the discount is an incentive to return?
- p08-a-10-s07 · Jules: Only three customer comments, not a proper comparison.
- p08-a-10-s08 · Farah: Let's say that plainly. I'll prepare the costs, and you can bring the panel and those comments.
- p08-a-10-s09 · Jules: Agreed. No promises about returns until we've worked through the numbers.

## 修订保护区告示以明确步道限制 · p08-a-11

主题：自然与环境；情景：明确保护区步道告示（reserve-path-protection-notice）。

- p08-a-11-s03 · Anika: Could we explain that repeated disturbance can harm the nesting birds?
- p08-a-11-s04 · George: Yes. That's clearer than just calling human activity harmful.
- p08-a-11-s05 · Anika: The rules also prohibit hunting anywhere in the reserve. That needs its own line.
- p08-a-11-s06 · George: I'll put that underneath, with the ranger's contact number for questions.
- p08-a-11-s07 · Anika: Good. Let's use short sentences, so people can read the sign before entering.

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The sign discussion translates a vague request into concrete path and reed-bed behaviour, explains disturbance to nesting birds and adds the reserve's own hunting prohibition. Short sentences and a ranger contact are the agreed endpoint; the restrictions are local fictional rules, not unsupported general law.

- p08-a-13-s03 · Wendy: I am. I've got some info from a travel blog, but it was posted last year.
- p08-a-13-s04 · Caleb: The boarding point can change. The ticket office has today's departures.
- p08-a-13-s05 · Wendy: Is that the blue building beside the water?

- p08-a-17-s01 · Marion: My hip is improving, but carrying groceries is still difficult.

- p08-a-17-s03 · Marion: That's kind, but my independence matters to me. I don't want you taking over jobs I can manage.
- p08-a-17-s04 · Sam: Understood. What would actually help?
- p08-a-17-s05 · Marion: A food shop on Saturday. I can write a list, and we can put the heavy things away together.
- p08-a-17-s06 · Sam: Let's do that. I'll come at eleven, and you can tell me if you need anything else.
- p08-a-17-s07 · Marion: Thank you. That feels like help rather than a takeover.

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The recovering mother declines an excessive offer of help, identifies grocery shopping as the actual difficulty and agrees to a Saturday visit at eleven while keeping control of other tasks. The Chinese goal accurately reflects autonomy and a limited practical agreement, not a medical recovery prediction.

## 约定先核实宗教画中的书本再改说明 · p08-a-19

主题：学习与技能；情景：核对宗教画展品说明（religious-painting-label-check）。

沟通目标：区分目录记载的画作主题、书本性质的推测与光线的象征解释，约定先向历史研究者核实书本后再修订说明

背景：博物馆志愿者检查一幅宗教画的说明草稿，讨论的是画作内容而非宗教真伪。

人物：Elena、Yusuf。

起草：author-phase08-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-a-19-s01 · Elena: The catalogue describes this as a vision of heaven and hell. Our label only says 'a strange landscape'.
- p08-a-19-s02 · Yusuf: Then we're leaving out the subject of the painting. We should use the catalogue's description.
- p08-a-19-s03 · Elena: What about the woman holding the book? Is it a holy text?
- p08-a-19-s04 · Yusuf: Possibly, but I can't read the cover. We shouldn't identify it just from the light around it.
- p08-a-19-s05 · Elena: A historian who studies this period is visiting tomorrow. I'll ask her.
- p08-a-19-s06 · Yusuf: Good. Different viewers might interpret the light as hope or protection.
- p08-a-19-s07 · Elena: So that part is an interpretation, not a confirmed fact about the object.
- p08-a-19-s08 · Yusuf: Exactly. Let's keep the documented subject and check the book before revising that sentence.

主题：消费与服务；情景：澄清住房援助申请的信息要求（housing-support-form-clarification）。

背景：来访者在一家虚构的住房援助服务处询问申请表，不涉及现实资格判断。

人物：Dina、Chris。

起草：author-phase08-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-a-20-s01 · Dina: I'm homeless at the moment and staying with a friend. Can I ask about this housing form?
- p08-a-20-s02 · Chris: Of course. Which part is unclear?
- p08-a-20-s03 · Dina: It asks for everyone in my household. Do I include the friend I'm staying with?
- p08-a-20-s04 · Chris: For this form, list the people who would move into the new home with you. We record temporary hosts separately.
- p08-a-20-s05 · Dina: And my income changes each week because my working hours vary.
- p08-a-20-s06 · Chris: The adviser will need the recent records listed on this sheet. You don't have to invent a fixed weekly figure.
- p08-a-20-s07 · Dina: Could I take the sheet and bring the records back before I finish the form?
- p08-a-20-s08 · Chris: Yes. I'll mark the two sections we discussed so the adviser can go through them with you.

The fictional service clarifies its own form's household convention and directs Dina to gather recent variable-income records instead of inventing a fixed figure. The ending agrees to take the checklist and return before completing the application, matching the Chinese goal without determining real-world eligibility.

## 约定先联系维修店检查继承的小提琴 · p08-a-21

主题：家庭与人际；情景：讨论继承乐器的纪念用途与检查（inherited-violin-memorial-plan）。

沟通目标：根据祖母信中的心愿讨论家庭聚会演奏，约定先联系维修店检查乐器，暂不作出演奏承诺

背景：姐弟整理祖母留下的小提琴，谈论怎样纪念她。

人物：Alice、Peter。

起草：author-phase08-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-a-21-s01 · Alice: Did you inherit the violin, or is it going to the music school?
- p08-a-21-s02 · Peter: She left it to me. Her letter says she'd like it to be played, not just displayed.
- p08-a-21-s03 · Alice: A short piece at the family gathering could honour that wish.
- p08-a-21-s04 · Peter: I'd like that, but it hasn't been used for years. I don't even know whether the strings are sound.
- p08-a-21-s05 · Alice: Then let's ask the repair shop to check it first. We don't need to promise a performance yet.
- p08-a-21-s06 · Peter: I'll call them tomorrow. Once it's ready, we can choose something she loved.

目标用法：

- inherit · v. · 继承遗留下来的财物；义项 sense-staged-ox-7faf737323257726-p08-a-21；来源 ox-7faf737323257726；证据 p08-a-21-s01；词形 inherit。
- honour · v. · 以行动尊重或实现他人的意愿（英式拼写）；义项 sense-staged-ox-46b08cc10b5d48ee-p08-a-21；来源 ox-46b08cc10b5d48ee；证据 p08-a-21-s03；词形 honour。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-a；整篇结论：accepted。

Complete version 2 reread confirms the title and goal now name the actual outcome: Peter will contact a repair shop tomorrow before committing to a family-gathering performance. The grandmother's letter grounds her wish, the unused violin's condition supplies a concrete obstacle, and repertoire selection stays conditional on readiness. No public concert or completed inspection is invented.

- ox-46b08cc10b5d48ee：accepted；In s03 honour that wish is the British-spelled verb for respectfully fulfilling the letter's desire that the instrument be played; the proposed family piece is conditional, not a pledged performance.（p08-a-21-s03）。

主题：家庭与人际；情景：确定志愿者致辞的幽默边界（volunteer-tribute-humor-boundary）。

- p08-a-22-s01 · Vera: We're here to honor Pat's years of work, so I don't want the speech to sound like a roast.
- p08-a-22-s02 · Noah: It can still have some humor. What about the time her dog joined the video meeting?
- p08-a-22-s03 · Vera: That was hilarious, and Pat tells the story herself. I'm less sure about the joke about her missing the bus.
- p08-a-22-s04 · Noah: Leave that out. A humorous story should make her feel included, not embarrassed.
- p08-a-22-s05 · Vera: I'll keep the dog story, then finish with what she did for the reading group.
- p08-a-22-s06 · Noah: Good. That gives people a smile and brings it back to why we're thanking her.

The American tribute-planning dialogue sets a respectful tone, selects a dog story Pat already tells herself, drops the potentially embarrassing missed-bus joke and ends with her reading-group contributions. Those explicit choices match both Chinese title and goal and give the humour a communicative purpose.

## 根据试演反馈简化魔术展示 · p08-a-24

主题：文娱与运动；情景：调整魔术道具展示的节奏（magic-prop-rehearsal-feedback）。

沟通目标：核对道具功能并根据观众视角反馈，约定去掉过长解释后重新试演

背景：魔术爱好者请朋友试看一个儿童活动用的小节目。

人物：Luca、Grace。

起草：author-phase08-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-a-24-s01 · Luca: Did the illusion work, or could you tell where the ball went?
- p08-a-24-s02 · Grace: I guessed instantly because you kept looking at the same corner.
- p08-a-24-s03 · Luca: The box is hollow, but the audience shouldn't see the opening from your seat.
- p08-a-24-s04 · Grace: I couldn't see it. Your eyes gave it away.
- p08-a-24-s05 · Luca: I was trying to impress everyone with a long story about an invisible bird.
- p08-a-24-s06 · Grace: The story leaves room for imagination, but it went on after the trick was already over.
- p08-a-24-s07 · Luca: So you weren't very impressed?
- p08-a-24-s08 · Grace: I liked the moment the ball vanished. Just let that happen without explaining every movement.
- p08-a-24-s09 · Luca: All right. I'll shorten the story and try it again from the start.

## 限制种子试验结论并计划增加重复试验 · p08-a-25

主题：研究与论证；情景：核对种子试验的推论边界（seed-trial-conclusion-limits）。

沟通目标：把一次观察结果限定为支持假设的初步迹象，约定重复试验而不声称已证明因果

背景：学生与辅导员检查一份种子发芽试验的课堂报告。

人物：Sana、Oliver。

起草：author-phase08-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-a-25-s01 · Sana: My hypothesis was that the seeds would sprout sooner in the warmer room.
- p08-a-25-s02 · Oliver: Were the containers identical apart from where you put them?
- p08-a-25-s03 · Sana: Yes. Same size, soil, and amount of water. But I only used one container in each room.
- p08-a-25-s04 · Oliver: Then what can you infer from the result?
- p08-a-25-s05 · Sana: The warmer room's seeds sprouted first. That's an indication that temperature may matter.
- p08-a-25-s06 · Oliver: Does it imply that temperature was definitely the cause?
- p08-a-25-s07 · Sana: No. The seeds could have differed, and we haven't repeated the test.
- p08-a-25-s08 · Oliver: So the practical implication is that we need more trials, not that growers should change their methods.
- p08-a-25-s09 · Sana: I'll revise the conclusion and plan several containers for each room next time.

目标用法：

- hypothesis · n. · 有待检验的假设；义项 sense-staged-ox-0e6d85198f0d5a2f-p08-a-25；来源 ox-0e6d85198f0d5a2f；证据 p08-a-25-s01；词形 hypothesis。
- identical · adj. · 完全相同的；义项 sense-staged-ox-48aa12ce1285b742-p08-a-25；来源 ox-48aa12ce1285b742；证据 p08-a-25-s02；词形 identical。
- infer · v. · 根据证据推断；义项 sense-staged-ox-0b175917fe657115-p08-a-25；来源 ox-0b175917fe657115；证据 p08-a-25-s04；词形 infer。
- indication · n. · 提示某种情况的迹象；义项 sense-staged-ox-ab4927038fbf5b7e-p08-a-25；来源 ox-ab4927038fbf5b7e；证据 p08-a-25-s05；词形 indication。
- imply · v. · 意味着或暗示某种结论；义项 sense-staged-ox-2297e456e9a97c08-p08-a-25；来源 ox-2297e456e9a97c08；证据 p08-a-25-s06；词形 imply。
- implication · n. · 某情况所带来的实际含义或影响；义项 sense-staged-ox-5a9c8a330a0ec9dd-p08-a-25；来源 ox-5a9c8a330a0ec9dd；证据 p08-a-25-s08；词形 implication。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-a；整篇结论：accepted。

Complete version 2 reread confirms s05 now reports seeds sprouting, the same event predicted by the hypothesis, rather than seeds merely appearing. The one-container-per-room limitation and possible seed differences lead to cautious inference, refusal to claim proven causation and a plan for further trials. The revised title explicitly marks those trials as planned, consistent with the ending and all six senses.

- ox-48aa12ce1285b742：accepted；In s02 identical describes the containers as the same apart from placement, clarified by equal size, soil and water; the qualification avoids claiming identical room conditions.（p08-a-25-s02）。
- ox-0b175917fe657115：accepted；In s04 infer from the result asks what conclusion the available observation permits, leading to a limited rather than definitive answer.（p08-a-25-s04）。

## 完成自助机读卡并转到柜台核验补卡 · p08-a-26

主题：消费与服务；情景：处理图书馆自助机的补卡限制（library-kiosk-card-assistance）。

- p08-a-26-s05 · Eva: Can I get an instant replacement here? The edge is cracked.
- p08-a-26-s06 · Malik: Not from this machine. The desk staff need to check your photo ID before issuing a new one.

## 试行轮流发言的工作坊规则 · p08-a-28

主题：工作与协作；情景：修订工作坊的参与规则（workshop-participation-guideline）。

沟通目标：把参与者反馈转化为轮流发言和允许澄清的规则，约定下一次试行后回顾效果

背景：两名培训组织者根据参与者反馈调整工作坊活动。

人物：Beth、Imran。

起草：author-phase08-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-a-28-s01 · Beth: People liked the group work, but some said they barely got a chance to interact.
- p08-a-28-s02 · Imran: Let's integrate that feedback into the next session rather than just thanking them for it.
- p08-a-28-s03 · Beth: What would you change? We can't force lively interaction by telling everyone to talk more.
- p08-a-28-s04 · Imran: One guideline could be that everyone gets a turn before anyone takes a second one.
- p08-a-28-s05 · Beth: Would that mean nobody can interrupt to ask a question?
- p08-a-28-s06 · Imran: Not quite. For instance, a quick request to explain an unfamiliar term should still be welcome.
- p08-a-28-s07 · Beth: Then let's distinguish clarification from taking over the discussion.
- p08-a-28-s08 · Imran: Agreed. We can implement the rule in the next workshop and ask whether it helped.
- p08-a-28-s09 · Beth: I'll explain it at the start, and we'll compare the feedback afterwards.

- interrupt · v. · 打断他人的发言；义项 sense-staged-ox-a87eba55e110b544-p08-a-28；来源 ox-a87eba55e110b544；证据 p08-a-28-s05；词形 interrupt。

## 暂停手册印刷并修正索引错页 · p08-a-29

- p08-a-29-s01 · Zoe: The index sends readers to page twelve for emergency contacts, but they're on page fourteen.

- p08-a-29-s03 · Zoe: Has the full print run started?
- p08-a-29-s04 · Nathan: No, this is just the proof. The ink is still wet on the test sheet.
- p08-a-29-s05 · Zoe: Please hold the rest. I'll update the references in the file.
- p08-a-29-s06 · Nathan: And I'll check each entry against the new proof before we print any more.
- p08-a-29-s07 · Zoe: Good. Let's leave the printer paused until we've both signed off on that version.

Their joint sign-off condition matches the Chinese goal; the ending does not claim corrections are already verified.

## 限制断网原因结论并安排隔离测试 · p08-a-30

主题：工作与协作；情景：核对断网调查的证据范围（network-fault-investigation-boundary）。

沟通目标：区分内部故障假设与已确认事实，约定单独测试更新并向同事说明当前未知原因

背景：技术支持同事讨论一次办公室网络中断后的测试记录。

人物：Dev、Lena。

起草：author-phase08-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-a-30-s01 · Dev: Initially, I thought it was an internal network fault because only our team reported it.
- p08-a-30-s02 · Lena: But we haven't asked the other teams yet. We should isolate the problem before naming a cause.
- p08-a-30-s03 · Dev: It happened just after the update, hence my suspicion that the update was involved.
- p08-a-30-s04 · Lena: The timing is a clue, not proof. Can we test the update on a spare machine?
- p08-a-30-s05 · Dev: Yes. Does the fact that it happened twice make another outage inevitable?
- p08-a-30-s06 · Lena: No. Repeating a fault doesn't mean it will inevitably happen every time, especially when the conditions differ.
- p08-a-30-s07 · Dev: I'll test one machine separately and record the network settings as well.
- p08-a-30-s08 · Lena: And I'll ask the other teams what they saw. For now, let's tell people the cause is still under investigation.

目标用法：

- initially · adv. · 起初；义项 sense-staged-ox-22d91deac54b8776-p08-a-30；来源 ox-22d91deac54b8776；证据 p08-a-30-s01；词形 initially。
- internal · adj. · 组织或系统内部的；义项 sense-staged-ox-4b3199b65943903a-p08-a-30；来源 ox-4b3199b65943903a；证据 p08-a-30-s01；词形 internal。
- isolate · v. · 将问题单独辨别或定位；义项 sense-staged-ox-99d49dbbb20ff794-p08-a-30；来源 ox-99d49dbbb20ff794；证据 p08-a-30-s02；词形 isolate。
- hence · adv. · 因此或由此；义项 sense-staged-ox-73af83c9d5a8d255-p08-a-30；来源 ox-73af83c9d5a8d255；证据 p08-a-30-s03；词形 hence。
- inevitable · adj. · 不可避免的；义项 sense-staged-ox-5eb9d254e3c91738-p08-a-30；来源 ox-5eb9d254e3c91738；证据 p08-a-30-s05；词形 inevitable。
- inevitably · adv. · 必然地；义项 sense-staged-ox-1fee1fefe11d09fa-p08-a-30；来源 ox-1fee1fefe11d09fa；证据 p08-a-30-s06；词形 inevitably。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The colleagues revise a team-local network hypothesis, treat update timing as a clue rather than proof, reject certainty about future outages and assign isolated testing plus other-team enquiries. Reporting the cause as still under investigation accurately achieves the Chinese goal without asserting a technical diagnosis.

- p08-a-31-s01 · Paula: I'm visiting headquarters on Tuesday. Who could explain how complaints reach the product team?

- p08-a-31-s06 · Jin: Good. Then label your notes as one team's experience, not a verdict on the whole company.

## 澄清虚构战争短剧的时间顺序并分段排练 · p08-a-32

主题：文娱与运动；情景：排练虚构战争短剧的被囚场景（historical-play-capture-rehearsal）。

- p08-a-32-s02 · Ivy: The invasion begins offstage. Your messenger brings the news to the council.
- p08-a-32-s03 · Oscar: Then why does my next line say the mayor is in jail? That seems too soon.
- p08-a-32-s04 · Ivy: That line belongs to the following scene, which is set a week later. You've turned two pages.
- p08-a-32-s05 · Oscar: Ah, I see. So I leave after warning the council, and we change the set before the prison scene.
- p08-a-32-s06 · Ivy: Exactly. Let's rehearse the message first, stop at your exit, and then work on the scene change.
- p08-a-32-s07 · Oscar: Ready. I'll start from the knock at the door.

This matches the Chinese sequencing goal and clearly confines invasion and imprisonment to a fictional school play.

## 选定航空展厅的短参观路线 · p08-a-33

主题：文娱与运动；情景：确定航空展参观路线（aviation-museum-route）。

- p08-a-33-s01 · Finn: We only have forty minutes. Is there time to see the old passenger jet?
- p08-a-33-s02 · Ada: Yes. It's in the far hall, about five minutes from here. The early aircraft are in this nearer room.
- p08-a-33-s03 · Finn: The large plane is what my daughter most wants to see. Should we go straight there?
- p08-a-33-s04 · Ada: I would. You can look at the smaller exhibits on the way back if you have time.
- p08-a-33-s05 · Finn: Then we'll take the corridor to the far hall first. Is that the entrance under the blue sign?
- p08-a-33-s06 · Ada: That's right. Follow it to the end, then turn left.

- jet · n. · 喷气式飞机；义项 sense-staged-ox-3e325c2075cae794-p08-a-33；来源 ox-3e325c2075cae794；证据 p08-a-33-s01；词形 jet。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The staff member uses the stated forty-minute allowance, a five-minute walk and the child's preference to recommend the distant passenger plane first. The visitor chooses that route and checks the blue-sign entrance, matching the Chinese goal without claiming every exhibit will fit.

## 先补充采访再修改校报报道 · p08-b-01

主题：工作与协作；情景：修订校报报道的证据与评价（student-news-fairness-revision）。

沟通目标：区分记者评价与已核实事实，约定补充采访并把指责改为可回答的问题

背景：英国一所学校的初级记者伊莫金与编辑哈立德核对一篇关于社团取消活动的报道。

人物：Imogen、Khalid。

起草：author-phase08-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-b-01-s01 · Imogen: This is my first piece of journalism. Could you check the opening before I send it?
- p08-b-01-s02 · Khalid: Of course. Being a junior reporter doesn't mean you have to get everything right alone.
- p08-b-01-s03 · Imogen: I wrote that the club leader doesn't care about the members. She cancelled their trip.
- p08-b-01-s04 · Khalid: That's a judgement about her motives. Have you asked why she cancelled it?
- p08-b-01-s05 · Imogen: No. I only spoke to two disappointed members. Should I make the whole article neutral?
- p08-b-01-s06 · Khalid: Keep their concerns, but be objective about what we know. The cancellation is a fact; her reasons aren't clear yet.
- p08-b-01-s07 · Imogen: I'll ask her what happened and change that accusation into a question about the decision.
- p08-b-01-s08 · Khalid: Good. Send me the revised opening after you've included her response, or said clearly that we're still waiting.

目标用法：

- journalism · n. · 新闻报道工作；义项 sense-staged-ox-c8cd5da5f61f8e92-p08-b-01；来源 ox-c8cd5da5f61f8e92；证据 p08-b-01-s01；词形 journalism。
- junior · adj. · 职位较低的；初级的；义项 sense-staged-ox-187b76d59cedcfac-p08-b-01；来源 ox-187b76d59cedcfac；证据 p08-b-01-s02；词形 junior。
- judgement · n. · 对某事或某人的判断；义项 sense-staged-ox-d74dd5f45699e039-p08-b-01；来源 ox-d74dd5f45699e039；证据 p08-b-01-s04；词形 judgement。
- neutral · adj. · 不偏向任何一方的；义项 sense-staged-ox-d35769dd7fda1a8e-p08-b-01；来源 ox-d35769dd7fda1a8e；证据 p08-b-01-s05；词形 neutral。
- objective · adj. · 客观的；依据事实的；义项 sense-staged-ox-de53e24a4877fd69-p08-b-01；来源 ox-de53e24a4877fd69；证据 p08-b-01-s06；词形 objective。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The editor distinguishes an unsupported claim about motives from the established cancellation; the reporter agrees to seek the leader’s response and revise the accusation. The ending explicitly allows a response-pending disclosure, so the Chinese goal promises a reporting plan rather than falsely claiming the interview is complete. British judgement and cancelled fit the setting.

## 修改模拟审判日志中的道德判断 · p08-b-02

主题：学习与技能；情景：区分模拟审判的证据与道德评价（mock-trial-journal-revision）。

沟通目标：依据虚构课堂材料区分证据判断和道德评价，约定重写学习日志的结论

背景：美国公民课上，艾丽西亚与老师讨论完全虚构的模拟审判任务；对话不提供法律意见。

人物：Alicia、Mr Brooks。

起草：author-phase08-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-b-02-s01 · Alicia: Our jury couldn't agree about the witness. Does that mean the exercise failed?
- p08-b-02-s02 · Mr Brooks: No. The task was to explain which evidence you trusted, not to reach a quick decision.
- p08-b-02-s03 · Alicia: In my journal, I said the witness was a bad person because he changed his story.
- p08-b-02-s04 · Mr Brooks: Can the evidence justify that judgment? A changed story may need explaining, but it doesn't tell us everything about a person.
- p08-b-02-s05 · Alicia: So I've turned a question about accuracy into a moral judgment.
- p08-b-02-s06 · Mr Brooks: Exactly. We can discuss justice without pretending that one classroom case gives us all the answers.
- p08-b-02-s07 · Alicia: I'll replace the last paragraph with the two contradictions we noticed and the questions we'd still need answered.
- p08-b-02-s08 · Mr Brooks: That will show your reasoning much more clearly.

目标用法：

- jury · n. · 陪审团；义项 sense-staged-ox-d4b1bcb0a53ca7f0-p08-b-02；来源 ox-d4b1bcb0a53ca7f0；证据 p08-b-02-s01；词形 jury。
- justice · n. · 公正；正义；义项 sense-staged-ox-96557415b2d236e8-p08-b-02；来源 ox-96557415b2d236e8；证据 p08-b-02-s06；词形 justice。
- judgment · n. · 判断；看法；义项 sense-staged-ox-14c15153d5f73c18-p08-b-02；来源 ox-14c15153d5f73c18；证据 p08-b-02-s04；词形 judgment。
- moral · adj. · 关于道德或是非的；义项 sense-staged-ox-74bb0a8c5aeb1f97-p08-b-02；来源 ox-74bb0a8c5aeb1f97；证据 p08-b-02-s05；词形 moral。
- justify · v. · 为判断提供正当理由；义项 sense-staged-ox-dddaf20eac38e295-p08-b-02；来源 ox-dddaf20eac38e295；证据 p08-b-02-s04；词形 justify。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The explicitly fictional US civics exercise develops from jury disagreement to an unjustified character accusation, then to a plan to list contradictions and open questions. The ending fulfills the stated journal-revision agreement without pretending to decide a real case. The American judgment spelling and distinct moral/evidentiary judgments are coherent.

- ox-96557415b2d236e8：accepted；In s06, justice is an abstract uncountable noun in “discuss justice”; the instructor refers to fairness and right outcomes, not an individual judge or a resolved legal case.（p08-b-02-s06）。
- ox-14c15153d5f73c18：accepted；In s04, “that judgment” refers back to Alicia’s claim that the witness is a bad person. It is a noun meaning an opinion or assessment, and its American spelling matches the US setting.（p08-b-02-s04）。
- ox-74bb0a8c5aeb1f97：accepted；In s05, moral attributively modifies judgment and contrasts character-based right/wrong assessment with accuracy of evidence. This is the adjective 道德的, not a story’s moral as a noun.（p08-b-02-s05）。

- p08-b-04-s05 · Rosa: I see. The catalogue price is based on mass production, then?
- p08-b-04-s06 · Dean: Yes, several hundred identical units. Your order would involve a separate setup.
- p08-b-04-s07 · Rosa: Please quote for twelve and show materials and labor separately. I'll send the exact dimensions this afternoon.

## 核对落地后的取车时间和出口路线 · p08-b-06

起草：author-phase08-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-b-06-s01 · Ivan: My flight is due in at six. How long after landing should I allow before collecting the car?
- p08-b-06-s02 · Ruth: You need time for baggage collection. Our desk stays open until ten, so there's no need to rush.
- p08-b-06-s03 · Ivan: I'll bring the booking confirmation. Do you need my driving licence as well?
- p08-b-06-s04 · Ruth: Yes, the original, please. We'll check the other documents listed in your confirmation when you arrive.
- p08-b-06-s05 · Ivan: Is the road out of the car park difficult? I won't know the navigation system yet.
- p08-b-06-s06 · Ruth: Stay in the left lane for the airport exit. There's a marked stopping area just beyond the barrier.
- p08-b-06-s07 · Ivan: Can I park nearby while I set the route? I'd rather not work it out while driving.
- p08-b-06-s08 · Ruth: Use that marked area. Set everything up there before you join the main road.
- p08-b-06-s09 · Ivan: Right. I'll collect my bags, come to your desk, and set the route before leaving the airport.

- p08-b-10-s06 · Felix: That gives us something to discuss beyond the plot. How long is it?
- p08-b-10-s07 · Yuna: Two hundred pages. We could read it before next month's meeting.
- p08-b-10-s08 · Felix: Let's choose it, then. I'll ask everyone to notice when the villagers first change their minds.

- p08-b-12-s03 · Ezra: The large hall has become more expensive. Likewise, the full-colour booklet costs more than we expected.
- p08-b-12-s04 · Ines: Then use the small room and a single-page handout. A modest first session would tell us whether people want another.
- p08-b-12-s05 · Ezra: Moreover, the small room already has the tables we need. We wouldn't have to hire any.
- p08-b-12-s06 · Ines: Let's set a total budget of one hundred pounds and limit the session to twelve people.
- p08-b-12-s07 · Ezra: I'll reserve the room within that limit. Can you prepare the handout and check volunteer availability?
- p08-b-12-s08 · Ines: Yes. I'll confirm both by Friday, and we won't promise a weekly programme yet.

## 重写歌词比喻后试唱 · p08-b-13

起草：author-phase08-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-b-13-s01 · Priya: Does the lyric in verse two work? I wrote, 'Your silence fills the station.'
- p08-b-13-s02 · Joe: It's a moving image, but I'm not sure who is waiting for whom.
- p08-b-13-s03 · Priya: The singer is waiting for a friend who has left town.
- p08-b-13-s04 · Joe: So the silence doesn't literally fill the building. It's a metaphor for feeling alone.
- p08-b-13-s05 · Priya: Exactly. Would 'I wait beside your empty seat' make that clearer?
- p08-b-13-s06 · Joe: Yes, and it fits the same part of the tune. We can lose the station image rather than explain it.
- p08-b-13-s07 · Priya: I'll use the empty-seat line. Let's sing that verse once and hear how it leads into the chorus.

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The collaborators identify ambiguity in their own station image, explain its loneliness metaphor, choose the more concrete empty-seat line and agree to test the verse aloud. The goal explicitly promises an agreement to sing rather than a completed performance, so the closing proposal is sufficient. The invented lyric excerpts support the discussion without attributing them to a real song.

- p08-b-14-s03 · Hana: What interests me is the making of the novel, especially the chapters she decided to cut.
- p08-b-14-s04 · Omar: We could ask whether cutting a chapter changed how she understood the main character.
- p08-b-14-s05 · Hana: That's better. It could start a lively discussion rather than produce a one-word answer.
- p08-b-14-s06 · Omar: And we don't need to use complicated literary terms to ask it.
- p08-b-14-s07 · Hana: I'll ask about the deleted chapter. Could you follow up about whether she changed the ending too?

The students move from a generic inspiration question to one about a deleted chapter’s effect on character, plus a follow-up about the ending. Assigning Hana and Omar separate questions fulfills the Chinese goal and prevents repetition. Making of the novel and literary terms are natural in this writing-workshop setting, not an isolated vocabulary list.

- p08-b-15-s05 · Nicole: It says to melt the butter first, then add them. Let's get everything ready before we turn on the heat.

## 约定不再拿朋友的面试失利开玩笑 · p08-b-19

主题：家庭与人际；情景：承认伤人玩笑并约定停止（friend-hurtful-joke-boundary）。

沟通目标：听取朋友对公开玩笑的不满，承认伤害并约定不再提起且先询问所需支持

起草：author-phase08-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-b-19-s01 · Kit: Can we talk about what you said at dinner? The joke about my failed interview felt nasty.
- p08-b-19-s02 · Ash: I thought we were laughing about it together. I didn't realise you were upset.
- p08-b-19-s03 · Kit: I told you because you're my mate, not because I wanted everyone else to hear it.
- p08-b-19-s04 · Ash: You're right. I should have kept it private. I'm sorry.
- p08-b-19-s05 · Kit: I know you've been a loyal friend. That's why I wanted to tell you rather than just avoid you.
- p08-b-19-s06 · Ash: I won't bring it up again. Would it help if I checked in about the next interview, or would you prefer not to discuss it?
- p08-b-19-s07 · Kit: Ask me privately first. Some days I want to talk; other days I don't.
- p08-b-19-s08 · Ash: Understood. I'll ask, and I'll respect it if the answer is no.

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Version 2 fully reread with every selected usage. The corrected title identifies Kit’s interview failure rather than claiming he made an error. Ash acknowledges a breach of privacy, apologizes, promises not to revive that joke and agrees to ask privately before discussing future interviews. Kit can still recognize past loyalty while setting this boundary; the British mate/realise usage and revised title/goal match the final agreement.

- ox-80bcce0037647efc：accepted；In s03, “you’re my mate” is a noun complement meaning trusted friend. The British informal register and private disclosure fit this noun candidate, not mating as a verb.（p08-b-19-s03）。

## 缩小对部长的采访问题并保留不同意见 · p08-b-24

主题：社会与公共事务；情景：明确虚构公共项目采访问题的范围（minister-interview-question-scope）。

沟通目标：依据给定公告拟定关于车辆用途的具体问题，并限定来信统计的适用范围

背景：校报编辑萨米与记者爱丽丝讨论一份完全虚构的公共救援项目公告；不涉及现实政治人物或选民。

人物：Alice、Sami。

起草：author-phase08-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-b-24-s01 · Alice: The minister has agreed to answer one question about the new emergency centre. What should we ask?
- p08-b-24-s02 · Sami: The announcement mentions military vehicles. I'd ask what role those vehicles will have and who will manage them.
- p08-b-24-s03 · Alice: We've received numerous letters about that. A majority support the project, so can I say the town supports it?
- p08-b-24-s04 · Sami: No. A majority of our letters isn't the same as a majority of residents.
- p08-b-24-s05 · Alice: And a minority of the writers want more information about noise and traffic.
- p08-b-24-s06 · Sami: Those concerns belong in the introduction too. We don't know how representative either group is.
- p08-b-24-s07 · Alice: I'll state the numbers as letters received, then ask about the vehicles' purpose and management.
- p08-b-24-s08 · Sami: That's a clear question. Keep any claim about the whole town out of the draft.

目标用法：

- minister · n. · 政府部长；义项 sense-staged-ox-07a9de56a1803656-p08-b-24；来源 ox-07a9de56a1803656；证据 p08-b-24-s01；词形 minister。
- military · adj. · 军用的；军队的；义项 sense-staged-ox-0fce19f92eb9bc2b-p08-b-24；来源 ox-0fce19f92eb9bc2b；证据 p08-b-24-s02；词形 military。
- majority · n. · 多数；大多数；义项 sense-staged-ox-19dae3a15684b18a-p08-b-24；来源 ox-19dae3a15684b18a；证据 p08-b-24-s03；词形 majority。
- minority · n. · 少数；义项 sense-staged-ox-6a45a120fad18641-p08-b-24；来源 ox-6a45a120fad18641；证据 p08-b-24-s05；词形 minority。
- numerous · adj. · 众多的；许多的；义项 sense-staged-ox-3e65bf19a43d8d54-p08-b-24；来源 ox-3e65bf19a43d8d54；证据 p08-b-24-s03；词形 numerous。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The reporter narrows a fictional minister interview to vehicle purpose and management, while the editor prevents extrapolating self-selected letters to the town’s residents. The minority’s concerns remain in the introduction and the final question/scope decision match the Chinese goal. No actual politician, real survey result or universal military-policy claim is introduced.

- ox-0fce19f92eb9bc2b：accepted；In s02, military modifies vehicles and identifies equipment connected to the armed forces. It is the adjective 军用/军队的, not the collective noun for the armed forces themselves.（p08-b-24-s02）。
- ox-19dae3a15684b18a：accepted；In s03, “a majority support the project” is a noun phrase referring back to the letters received. Plural agreement is natural for the represented group; s04 explicitly prevents expanding the denominator to residents.（p08-b-24-s03）。
- ox-6a45a120fad18641：accepted；In s05, “a minority of the writers” is a noun phrase identifying the smaller share of correspondents who seek information. It is a numerical minority, not an unsupported demographic categorization.（p08-b-24-s05）。

## 选定只到开放庭院的历史步行路线 · p08-b-25

主题：出行与住宿；情景：核对历史步行的参观边界（heritage-walk-visit-boundaries）。

沟通目标：确认纪念碑与清真寺庭院路线及室内限制，选定不进入祈祷区的参观安排

背景：游客法拉向虚构小镇旅游处工作人员丹尼尔询问下午的历史步行路线。

人物：Farah、Daniel。

起草：author-phase08-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-b-25-s01 · Farah: Can we see the riverside monument and the old mosque on the same walk?
- p08-b-25-s02 · Daniel: Yes. The public path passes the monument first, then leads to the mosque's outer courtyard.
- p08-b-25-s03 · Farah: Are visitors allowed inside this afternoon? We don't want to interrupt anything.
- p08-b-25-s04 · Daniel: The notice from the hosts says the prayer area is closed to tours today. The courtyard remains open until four.
- p08-b-25-s05 · Farah: That's fine. We're interested in the outside of the building. Is there a sign showing where visitors should stop?
- p08-b-25-s06 · Daniel: There is, at the inner gate. Please follow it, and ask the host there if anything is unclear.
- p08-b-25-s07 · Farah: We'll take that route and stay in the open courtyard. Could you mark the public path on our map?
- p08-b-25-s08 · Daniel: Certainly. I've marked the turn after the monument and the return path to the square.

目标用法：

- monument · n. · 纪念碑；义项 sense-staged-ox-133caa4549ec8762-p08-b-25；来源 ox-133caa4549ec8762；证据 p08-b-25-s01；词形 monument。
- mosque · n. · 清真寺；义项 sense-staged-ox-3751d78c1d929f5f-p08-b-25；来源 ox-3751d78c1d929f5f；证据 p08-b-25-s01；词形 mosque。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The route connects the riverside monument to the mosque’s outer courtyard; the fictional hosts’ notice closes the prayer area and sets a courtyard closing time. Farah explicitly chooses the public route and courtyard only, and Daniel marks it. The title and goal are achieved without presenting one site’s restrictions as rules for all mosques.

- ox-133caa4549ec8762：accepted；In s01, “the riverside monument” is a countable commemorative landmark on a heritage walk, reinforced by its role as the first stop and later map turning point. The Chinese 纪念碑 reading fits that concrete destination.（p08-b-25-s01）。
- ox-3751d78c1d929f5f：accepted；In s01, “the old mosque” is a countable religious-building noun. The courtyard, prayer area and host-controlled visitor boundary substantiate 清真寺 while respecting this fictional site’s access rules.（p08-b-25-s01）。

## 明确无障碍底线后询问场租报价 · p08-b-26

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：确定活动场租谈判的边界（room-hire-negotiation-boundary）。

沟通目标：确认无障碍入口不可取消，约定以缩短时段换取报价而不作租用承诺

背景：社区活动组织者米洛与黛比商量如何向场馆询问更合适的租用条件。

人物：Milo、Debbie。

起草：author-phase08-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-b-26-s01 · Milo: Could you negotiate a lower room price? The quote is beyond our budget.
- p08-b-26-s02 · Debbie: I can ask. Would a shorter booking give us a useful starting point for the negotiation?
- p08-b-26-s03 · Milo: Yes, but we can't move upstairs. A step-free entrance is a necessity for several participants.
- p08-b-26-s04 · Debbie: Agreed. Saving money isn't a reason to exclude them.
- p08-b-26-s05 · Milo: The manager sounded firm about the price. Nevertheless, asking about two hours instead of three seems reasonable.
- p08-b-26-s06 · Debbie: Email is the simplest means of getting a clear written answer. I'll explain the shorter session and the access requirement.
- p08-b-26-s07 · Milo: Please ask for a revised quote, not a confirmed booking. We still need to check the total.
- p08-b-26-s08 · Debbie: Understood. I'll bring the reply back to you before we make any commitment.

目标用法：

- negotiate · v. · 协商；谈判以达成条件；义项 sense-staged-ox-ce419b34ccb22181-p08-b-26；来源 ox-ce419b34ccb22181；证据 p08-b-26-s01；词形 negotiate。
- negotiation · n. · 协商；谈判；义项 sense-staged-ox-54677334af9f994b-p08-b-26；来源 ox-54677334af9f994b；证据 p08-b-26-s02；词形 negotiation。
- means · n. · 方式；手段；义项 sense-staged-ox-3ac75adb65b425bd-p08-b-26；来源 ox-3ac75adb65b425bd；证据 p08-b-26-s06；词形 means。
- necessity · n. · 必不可少的事物或条件；义项 sense-staged-ox-71db5340cfa518f9-p08-b-26；来源 ox-71db5340cfa518f9；证据 p08-b-26-s03；词形 necessity。
- nevertheless · adv. · 尽管如此；然而；义项 sense-staged-ox-12013a108e08e899-p08-b-26；来源 ox-12013a108e08e899；证据 p08-b-26-s05；词形 nevertheless。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The organizers preserve step-free access as essential, propose shortening the booking from three hours to two and choose email for a written revised quote. They explicitly withhold a booking commitment pending review of the total. The Chinese goal accurately represents negotiation preparation rather than a successful price reduction or a reservation.

- ox-3ac75adb65b425bd：accepted；In s06, “the simplest means of getting a clear written answer” uses the noun means for a method, with email as that method. Its final s belongs to the lemma and is not a third-person verb ending.（p08-b-26-s06）。
- ox-71db5340cfa518f9：accepted；In s03, “a step-free entrance is a necessity” uses a countable noun complement for an indispensable condition. The following refusal to exclude participants makes the essential rather than optional status clear.（p08-b-26-s03）。

## 约定先恢复短时间钢琴练习 · p08-b-27

主题：文娱与运动；情景：商定恢复钢琴练习的一小步（piano-practice-restart-plan）。

沟通目标：讨论挫败感并约定一段可坚持的练习及分享安排

背景：成年人佩特拉因停练许久而沮丧，与朋友约瑟夫讨论恢复钢琴练习。

人物：Petra、Josef。

起草：author-phase08-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-b-27-s01 · Petra: I sit at the piano and expect to master the whole piece again. Then I stop after the first mistake.
- p08-b-27-s02 · Josef: Would working on just four bars make it easier to begin?
- p08-b-27-s03 · Petra: Maybe. I don't have much motivation after work.
- p08-b-27-s04 · Josef: What used to motivate you: playing for someone, or hearing your own progress?
- p08-b-27-s05 · Petra: Hearing a phrase become smoother. I could spend ten minutes on those four bars instead of struggling through everything.
- p08-b-27-s06 · Josef: That sounds easier to maintain than an hour you never have time for.
- p08-b-27-s07 · Petra: I'll try ten minutes after dinner on Monday and Wednesday. Could I play the phrase for you next weekend?
- p08-b-27-s08 · Josef: I'd like that. We can listen for what feels easier, not count mistakes.

目标用法：

- motivate · v. · 激励；使愿意行动；义项 sense-staged-ox-5a4c294079b51364-p08-b-27；来源 ox-5a4c294079b51364；证据 p08-b-27-s04；词形 motivate。
- motivation · n. · 动力；积极性；义项 sense-staged-ox-967cf77042e48187-p08-b-27；来源 ox-967cf77042e48187；证据 p08-b-27-s03；词形 motivation。
- master · v. · 熟练掌握；义项 sense-staged-ox-79670c12f78c7ace-p08-b-27；来源 ox-79670c12f78c7ace；证据 p08-b-27-s01；词形 master。
- maintain · v. · 维持；坚持；义项 sense-staged-ox-c90cfbfbf2176ecd-p08-b-27；来源 ox-c90cfbfbf2176ecd；证据 p08-b-27-s06；词形 maintain。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Petra’s unrealistic whole-piece expectation and lack of energy lead to a four-bar, ten-minute plan on two specified evenings. Josef agrees to hear the phrase next weekend without counting mistakes. The ending satisfies the modest practice-and-sharing goal and does not claim restored mastery or a proven psychological intervention.

绑定文件 SHA-256：48ebd9c207cc6ebda3b627c1f78b7072de1fc20d2e48a6c25592253fa3cc811f

- ox-5a4c294079b51364：accepted；In s04, motivate is a transitive verb after “used to”, with you as object; the alternatives ask what formerly encouraged Petra to practice. Her answer identifies audible improvement as the incentive.（p08-b-27-s04）。
- ox-967cf77042e48187：accepted；In s03, motivation is an uncountable noun after “much”, describing little drive to practice after work. It is the desire/energy sense, not an allegation of hidden motives.（p08-b-27-s03）。
- ox-79670c12f78c7ace：accepted；In s01, “to master the whole piece again” uses the verb candidate in an infinitival clause and means gain skilled command of the music. The dialogue challenges that immediate expectation rather than saying it has been achieved.（p08-b-27-s01）。
- ox-c90cfbfbf2176ecd：accepted；In s06, “easier to maintain” uses the infinitive verb with the proposed short practice routine understood as its object. The contrast with an impossible hour supports continuing/sustaining a habit, not maintaining a machine or an argumentative claim.（p08-b-27-s06）。

## 核对海报方格数量后标出留白 · p08-b-28

主题：学习与技能；情景：核算海报方格与打印数量（poster-grid-math-check）。

沟通目标：纠正方格总数的计算，确认多张海报的材料数量并约定标出边缘留白

背景：学生奥斯卡与麦准备三张有相同布局的班级展示海报。

人物：Oscar、Mai。

起草：author-phase08-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-b-28-s01 · Oscar: There are four columns and three rows, so I wrote seven spaces on each poster.
- p08-b-28-s02 · Mai: You need to multiply four by three, not add them. Each poster has twelve spaces.
- p08-b-28-s03 · Oscar: Right. We need multiple copies of the poster, one for each of the three classrooms.
- p08-b-28-s04 · Mai: Then twelve spaces on each of three posters means thirty-six small cards altogether.
- p08-b-28-s05 · Oscar: That's logical. I'll count out thirty-six blank cards before we start writing.
- p08-b-28-s06 · Mai: Leave a margin around the outside, though. The first layout went right to the edge.
- p08-b-28-s07 · Oscar: Could you use a pencil to mark that? The marker makes a line we can't rub out.
- p08-b-28-s08 · Mai: Yes. I'll mark the border in pencil on the first poster, and we can check it before copying the layout.

目标用法：

- multiply · v. · 做乘法；相乘；义项 sense-staged-ox-50cfc672cb33dd96-p08-b-28；来源 ox-50cfc672cb33dd96；证据 p08-b-28-s02；词形 multiply。
- multiple · adj. · 多个的；多份的；义项 sense-staged-ox-73141bb24e2d23e4-p08-b-28；来源 ox-73141bb24e2d23e4；证据 p08-b-28-s03；词形 multiple。

- margin · n. · 页面或版面边缘的留白；义项 sense-staged-ox-856f5bec12183d2b-p08-b-28；来源 ox-856f5bec12183d2b；证据 p08-b-28-s06；词形 margin。
- marker · n. · 记号笔；义项 sense-staged-ox-51ed4f5977697546-p08-b-28；来源 ox-51ed4f5977697546；证据 p08-b-28-s07；词形 marker。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Mai corrects addition to multiplication: four columns times three rows gives twelve places, and three posters require thirty-six cards. They then agree on erasable pencil margins before replicating the layout. The calculations, materials decision and planned border markings support the title and goal; the original seven-space claim is explicitly corrected.

## 让新同事先澄清不明要求 · p08-b-29

主题：工作与协作；情景：澄清新员工提问与执行的边界（new-starter-questioning-norm）。

沟通目标：说明团队允许质疑不清楚的流程，同时区分必须遵守的安全要求，并确定求助对象

背景：新入职的西蒙向团队负责人艾达询问内部工作习惯。

人物：Simon、Ada。

起草：author-phase08-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-b-29-s01 · Simon: As someone newly appointed to the team, I'm not sure when it's acceptable to question a process.
- p08-b-29-s02 · Ada: Asking why is the norm here, especially if a request isn't clear.
- p08-b-29-s03 · Simon: I had the notion that new staff should just obey instructions until they'd been here for a while.
- p08-b-29-s04 · Ada: You must follow the safety rules, of course. But you shouldn't pretend to understand an unclear task.
- p08-b-29-s05 · Simon: My old office rarely discussed procedures. Do teams handle that differently nowadays?
- p08-b-29-s06 · Ada: Some do, but I can only speak for this one. We would rather answer a question than discover a preventable mistake later.
- p08-b-29-s07 · Simon: Then I'll bring the unclear delivery form to you before I complete it.
- p08-b-29-s08 · Ada: Please do. We'll check the requirement together, and I can show you who owns that process.

目标用法：

- norm · n. · 通常的做法；惯例；义项 sense-staged-ox-afb1b0a84f579297-p08-b-29；来源 ox-afb1b0a84f579297；证据 p08-b-29-s02；词形 norm。
- notion · n. · 想法；观念；义项 sense-staged-ox-d35053b00101114c-p08-b-29；来源 ox-d35053b00101114c；证据 p08-b-29-s03；词形 notion。
- nowadays · adv. · 如今；现今；义项 sense-staged-ox-540173b8d23423a8-p08-b-29；来源 ox-540173b8d23423a8；证据 p08-b-29-s05；词形 nowadays。
- newly · adv. · 新近地；最近；义项 sense-staged-ox-09ec5df18d771cab-p08-b-29；来源 ox-09ec5df18d771cab；证据 p08-b-29-s01；词形 newly。
- obey · v. · 服从；遵守；义项 sense-staged-ox-9e82c00420828c21-p08-b-29；来源 ox-9e82c00420828c21；证据 p08-b-29-s03；词形 obey。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Ada confines the questioning norm to this team, distinguishes safety rules from unclear tasks and agrees to help Simon with the delivery form before he completes it. The title and goal are met because Ada is the immediate help contact, while the process owner will be shown later. The dialogue avoids an unsupported claim that every modern workplace has the same culture.

## 先核对规则再筹备慈善抽奖 · p08-b-30

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：分工筹备慈善抽奖（charity-raffle-planning）。

沟通目标：确定筹款用途、奖品和筹备分工，并约定售票前核对活动规则

背景：社区志愿者伊芙与哈立尔讨论一项尚未启动的虚构小型慈善抽奖。

人物：Eve、Khalil。

起草：author-phase08-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-b-30-s01 · Eve: Could a small lottery help us raise money for the reading room?
- p08-b-30-s02 · Khalil: Perhaps, but we need to check the rules before we sell any tickets.
- p08-b-30-s03 · Eve: The bookshop has offered a book hamper as a prize. I'd like all the proceeds to go towards new reading lamps.
- p08-b-30-s04 · Khalil: That gives us a clear purpose. I'll ask the community centre which rules apply and who needs to approve the event.
- p08-b-30-s05 · Eve: I'll confirm the donated prize and draft a notice, but I won't print tickets yet.
- p08-b-30-s06 · Khalil: Good. Once we have the guidance, we can decide whether to go ahead and set a date.

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The volunteers identify the reading lamps as the fundraising purpose and the offered book hamper as prize, then split rule-checking and preliminary prize/notice preparation. They explicitly postpone ticket printing/sales and the go-ahead decision pending guidance. The goal accurately captures this conditional planning and does not claim legal permission, ticket sales or money raised; the title’s rule-first emphasis is supported for proceeding with the event.

## 约定修改健康活动传单并交讲者核对 · p08-b-31

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：商定健康活动传单修订及复核安排（health-event-leaflet-respectful-draft-review）。

沟通目标：商定修改传单的责备性措辞并补正讲者称谓和免费信息，约定由讲者复核健康内容后再印刷

背景：社区健康活动编辑萨拉与志愿者托比核对一份虚构活动宣传传单，不提供个人健康建议。

- p08-b-31-s01 · Sarah: The leaflet says, 'Bad choices cause obesity.' I don't think we should print that.
- p08-b-31-s02 · Toby: Agreed. It blames people and turns a complex topic into a slogan.
- p08-b-31-s03 · Sarah: Could the title be 'Nutrition questions: an open conversation' instead?
- p08-b-31-s04 · Toby: Yes. We can say the event welcomes adults living with obesity, without making promises about results.
- p08-b-31-s05 · Sarah: The speaker's title should be 'registered dietitian', not 'doctor'. And the session is free; that needs to be visible.
- p08-b-31-s06 · Toby: I'll correct those details and remove the sentence about bad choices.
- p08-b-31-s07 · Sarah: Then please send the health information to the speaker for review before anything goes to print.
- p08-b-31-s08 · Toby: I will. I'll mark this as a draft until we've had that review.

The speakers agree to remove blame-based wording, use a neutral nutrition title, correct the registered-dietitian title and make the free-session detail visible. Toby will keep it in draft until the speaker reviews the health content; the metadata correctly promises those revisions and review arrangements, not completed approval or printing.

- p08-c-03-s03 · Ivo: This map shows the musicians here and the food stalls beside the gate.
- p08-c-03-s04 · Maya: The stalls would occupy the whole entrance. That would leave no passage for people entering the square.
- p08-c-03-s05 · Ivo: Could we put them along the far wall instead?
- p08-c-03-s06 · Maya: Yes, if the marked emergency route stays clear. Show both on the revised map.
- p08-c-03-s07 · Ivo: I'll move the stalls on the plan and send the new map with the application.

## 承认口音笑话的冒犯并约定停止 · p08-c-04

主题：家庭与人际；情景：为取笑口音道歉并确认边界（accent-joke-apology-boundary）。

沟通目标：听取朋友对口音笑话的感受，承认影响并同意不再这样开玩笑

背景：两位朋友私下谈论聚会时的一句笑话。

人物：Ben、Asha。

起草：author-phase08-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-c-04-s01 · Ben: I could tell you were upset last night. Can we talk openly about what happened?
- p08-c-04-s02 · Asha: You copied my accent while everyone was listening. I found that offensive.
- p08-c-04-s03 · Ben: I didn't mean to offend you, but I can see that I did.
- p08-c-04-s04 · Asha: Saying it was a joke doesn't take away the offence. I felt singled out.
- p08-c-04-s05 · Ben: You're right. I'm sorry, and I won't make jokes about your accent again.
- p08-c-04-s06 · Asha: Thank you. That's what I needed you to understand.

目标用法：

- openly · adv. · 坦诚地；义项 sense-staged-ox-8f2d5ca1c306dce0-p08-c-04；来源 ox-8f2d5ca1c306dce0；证据 p08-c-04-s01；词形 openly。
- offensive · adj. · 冒犯人的；义项 sense-staged-ox-5f2df1df77df41a6-p08-c-04；来源 ox-5f2df1df77df41a6；证据 p08-c-04-s02；词形 offensive。
- offend · v. · 冒犯；义项 sense-staged-ox-7170603ffc61ffad-p08-c-04；来源 ox-7170603ffc61ffad；证据 p08-c-04-s03；词形 offend。
- offence · n. · 冒犯造成的伤害或不快；义项 sense-staged-ox-7f6de919064a6973-p08-c-04；来源 ox-7f6de919064a6973；证据 p08-c-04-s04；词形 offence。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Ben invites a private discussion, Asha identifies both the accent imitation and its emotional effect, and Ben accepts responsibility and promises to stop. Asha’s final acknowledgment completes the apology-and-boundary goal without pretending the initial lack of intent erases harm; British offence spelling is consistent.

- ox-5f2df1df77df41a6：accepted；In s02 found that offensive evaluates the imitation as hurtful; the adjective is an object complement and fits 冒犯人的.（p08-c-04-s02）。
- ox-7170603ffc61ffad：accepted；In s03 mean to offend you uses the transitive verb for causing hurt, and the admission I did confirms that impact despite denied intent.（p08-c-04-s03）。
- ox-7f6de919064a6973：accepted；In s04 the offence refers to the hurt already described in s02, reinforced by I felt singled out; this noun is the interpersonal 不快 sense rather than a criminal act.（p08-c-04-s04）。

- p08-c-05-s02 · Ms Carter: No. The offender is the person who committed the offense. In this case, that person is Alex.
- p08-c-05-s03 · Luis: So shoplifting belongs under 'offense', and Alex belongs under 'offender'.
- p08-c-05-s04 · Ms Carter: Exactly. What penalty does this fictional case say the court imposed?
- p08-c-05-s05 · Luis: A fine. I'll put that in the last column, not the person's name.
- p08-c-05-s06 · Ms Carter: Good. Keep your answer to the facts given in the case.

- offender · n. · 违法者；义项 sense-staged-ox-7e240b8008dd0bfd-p08-c-05；来源 ox-7e240b8008dd0bfd；证据 p08-c-05-s02；词形 offender。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Teacher and learner distinguish Alex, shoplifting, and a fine in the three case-table columns. Luis corrects the person/action confusion and identifies where the punishment belongs, satisfying the title and goal. The case is explicitly fictional and American-English offense is used consistently, so the dialogue does not assert a real legal sentence.

- p08-c-06-s05 · Clara: Can we see the whole stage from there?

- p08-c-06-s07 · Clara: Then I'll take those two seats for the afternoon, please.

起草：author-phase08-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-c-08-s06 · Owen: Correct. We can proceed with a single test page now.

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：起草游乐场旁道路方案的具体反对意见（playground-road-objection-submission）。

- p08-c-09-s04 · Samir: No. My prime concern is children crossing between the gate and the play area.
- p08-c-09-s05 · Nell: Then let's make that clear. Otherwise, the council may think the opposition is to the whole improvement plan.
- p08-c-09-s06 · Samir: I'll mark the crossing point on the map and suggest using the existing service entrance.

The residents distinguish objection to the access road from resistance to all park improvements. Children’s crossing risk leads to marking a map and proposing the existing entrance, and the closing agreement is to submit those specifics. This matches the narrow Chinese goal and avoids claiming that the fictional council has accepted the alternative.

## 更正虚构国家制度图并分开政策立场 · p08-c-10

主题：学习与技能；情景：区分总统制结构与进步政策立场（fictional-government-chart-revision）。

沟通目标：根据课堂案例补画议会，并把政策立场说明从政府结构图移到单独栏

背景：学生核对关于虚构国家的公民课图示。

人物：Yuki、Leo。

起草：author-phase08-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-c-10-s01 · Yuki: My chart shows the president making every law. The case says this is a presidential system.
- p08-c-10-s02 · Leo: But it also says parliament debates and passes laws. You've left that branch out.
- p08-c-10-s03 · Yuki: I thought the description of the president as progressive meant the office had extra powers.
- p08-c-10-s04 · Leo: That describes her support for social reform, not the structure of the government.
- p08-c-10-s05 · Yuki: Then I'll add parliament and put her policy views in a separate box.
- p08-c-10-s06 · Leo: Let's check the arrows against the case notes before we hand it in.

目标用法：

- presidential · adj. · 总统制的；义项 sense-staged-ox-e36cc709314f1efa-p08-c-10；来源 ox-e36cc709314f1efa；证据 p08-c-10-s01；词形 presidential。
- parliament · n. · 议会；义项 sense-staged-ox-ebc305d465a7d565-p08-c-10；来源 ox-ebc305d465a7d565；证据 p08-c-10-s02；词形 parliament。
- progressive · adj. · 支持社会改革的；义项 sense-staged-ox-22612369b04d2e48-p08-c-10；来源 ox-22612369b04d2e48；证据 p08-c-10-s03；词形 progressive。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Yuki’s two explicit misconceptions are corrected: the fictional president does not make every law, and reform views do not add formal powers. The proposed chart repair adds parliament and separates policy views, followed by checking arrows against case notes. The title/goal concern this conceptual revision, and the government details are bounded to the fictional classroom case.

- ox-e36cc709314f1efa：accepted；In s01 presidential modifies system to identify the case’s form of government. The adjective itself does not support Yuki’s initial extra claim about all lawmaking, which s02 correctly challenges.（p08-c-10-s01）。
- ox-ebc305d465a7d565：accepted；In s02 parliament is the legislative institution that debates and passes laws in the supplied fictional case; the institutional noun fits 议会 and the omitted chart branch.（p08-c-10-s02）。

## 改练变速跑位以突破对手预判 · p08-c-11

主题：文娱与运动；情景：调整足球进攻训练的节奏与跑位（football-attacking-practice-adjustment）。

沟通目标：根据对手容易预判跑位的问题，同意先在半场练习变速和不同跑动路线

背景：训练结束后，队员与教练讨论下一段练习。

人物：Milo、Tessa。

起草：author-phase08-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-c-11-s01 · Milo: My opponent seems to know where I'll run before I move.
- p08-c-11-s02 · Tessa: Your runs have become predictable. You always head straight down the right side of the pitch.
- p08-c-11-s03 · Milo: Should I just try to run faster?
- p08-c-11-s04 · Tessa: Not all the time. A change of pace can help, especially if you move inside first.
- p08-c-11-s05 · Milo: I'd like to overcome that habit, but I need a chance to try without a full match going on.
- p08-c-11-s06 · Tessa: Then we'll use half the field and practise two different runs. I'll defend at first.
- p08-c-11-s07 · Milo: All right. I'll start slowly and change speed after the first pass.

目标用法：

- opponent · n. · 对手；义项 sense-staged-ox-7dd584d71450bcb7-p08-c-11；来源 ox-7dd584d71450bcb7；证据 p08-c-11-s01；词形 opponent。
- predictable · adj. · 可预料的；义项 sense-staged-ox-ff4687ff10d5b7c3-p08-c-11；来源 ox-ff4687ff10d5b7c3；证据 p08-c-11-s02；词形 predictable。
- pitch · n. · 足球场；义项 sense-staged-ox-caa78821ed8d8fec-p08-c-11；来源 ox-caa78821ed8d8fec；证据 p08-c-11-s02；词形 pitch。
- pace · n. · 速度或节奏；义项 sense-staged-ox-17f0a98219be8463-p08-c-11；来源 ox-17f0a98219be8463；证据 p08-c-11-s04；词形 pace。
- overcome · v. · 克服；义项 sense-staged-ox-1edacd9f44cc1758-p08-c-11；来源 ox-1edacd9f44cc1758；证据 p08-c-11-s05；词形 overcome。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The coach explains why the same right-side run is easy to anticipate, rejects simply increasing speed, and offers two varied runs on half a field. Milo ends with a concrete slow-start/speed-change plan, matching the goal of agreeing to practice rather than claiming the habit is already cured. Pitch/practise are consistent British usage.

## 暂缓扩展工作坊并先解决场地障碍 · p08-c-12

主题：工作与协作；情景：根据尚未结束的试办确定扩展条件（workshop-pilot-expansion-check）。

沟通目标：区分初步反馈与最终结果，决定先联系无障碍场地并在本阶段结束后复核扩展计划

人物：Amira、Kit。

起草：author-phase08-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-c-12-s01 · Amira: The first two sessions look promising. Can we announce more workshops?
- p08-c-12-s02 · Kit: Not yet. The trial is ongoing, and we haven't heard from the people who stopped attending.
- p08-c-12-s03 · Amira: Overall, the feedback we do have is positive.
- p08-c-12-s04 · Kit: True, but the stairs are still an obstacle for several people. More sessions in the same room won't solve that.
- p08-c-12-s05 · Amira: I'm optimistic about the library room. They have step-free access.
- p08-c-12-s06 · Kit: Let's ask about availability. The outcome of this trial should guide expansion, not just the first week's excitement.
- p08-c-12-s07 · Amira: I'll contact the library and collect the missing feedback.
- p08-c-12-s08 · Kit: Then we'll review the plan when this phase ends next Friday.

- promising · adj. · 有希望取得好结果的；义项 sense-staged-ox-f85d9ba07cd32a7e-p08-c-12；来源 ox-f85d9ba07cd32a7e；证据 p08-c-12-s01；词形 promising。

- overall · adv. · 总体上；义项 sense-staged-ox-594358a91e605fd7-p08-c-12；来源 ox-594358a91e605fd7；证据 p08-c-12-s03；词形 overall。
- obstacle · n. · 障碍；义项 sense-staged-ox-4ba88e272d8c25ee-p08-c-12；来源 ox-4ba88e272d8c25ee；证据 p08-c-12-s04；词形 obstacle。
- optimistic · adj. · 乐观的；义项 sense-staged-ox-91f199467e769b8c-p08-c-12；来源 ox-91f199467e769b8c；证据 p08-c-12-s05；词形 optimistic。
- outcome · n. · 结果；义项 sense-staged-ox-f7e70ee3c2db0144-p08-c-12；来源 ox-f7e70ee3c2db0144；证据 p08-c-12-s06；词形 outcome。
- phase · n. · 阶段；义项 sense-staged-ox-707c54a4e9e28751-p08-c-12；来源 ox-707c54a4e9e28751；证据 p08-c-12-s08；词形 phase。

Early positive feedback is explicitly distinguished from an unfinished trial and missing responses. The stairs provide a concrete access barrier; the speakers assign a library availability check and feedback collection, then schedule review for next Friday. The limited ending matches postponement and preparatory work, not successful expansion or a confirmed room booking.

## 选定低调补片以保留纪念外套 · p08-c-13

主题：消费与服务；情景：修补有纪念意义的外套外层（keepsake-coat-patch-repair）。

背景：顾客带来有纪念意义的旧外套咨询修补。

人物：Faye、Imran。

起草：author-phase08-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-c-13-s01 · Faye: Can you preserve the original fabric? This was my father's coat.
- p08-c-13-s02 · Imran: I'll keep as much as I can. Only the outer layer is torn; the lining is intact.
- p08-c-13-s03 · Faye: Would a patch be very noticeable?
- p08-c-13-s04 · Imran: I can sew a dark one underneath the tear. The stitching will show a little, but it won't cover the whole pocket.
- p08-c-13-s05 · Faye: That's fine. I'd rather see a small repair than replace the fabric.
- p08-c-13-s06 · Imran: Then I'll repair it that way. You can collect it on Thursday.

- preserve · v. · 保留或保护原有事物；义项 sense-staged-ox-f20e5c3e700ef11f-p08-c-13；来源 ox-f20e5c3e700ef11f；证据 p08-c-13-s01；词形 preserve。
- outer · adj. · 外层的；义项 sense-staged-ox-4e2a2aefdf98aa7f-p08-c-13；来源 ox-4e2a2aefdf98aa7f；证据 p08-c-13-s02；词形 outer。
- patch · n. · 补衣物的布片；义项 sense-staged-ox-694aca97ed6a5a6b-p08-c-13；来源 ox-694aca97ed6a5a6b；证据 p08-c-13-s03；词形 patch。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The repairer distinguishes torn outer cloth from intact lining and offers an underneath patch while honestly acknowledging visible stitches. Faye chooses retaining the original fabric over replacement, and Thursday collection completes the method-and-pickup agreement in the Chinese goal.

- p08-c-14-s04 · Rina: These pointed shoes suit the period, but try walking in them before you decide.
- p08-c-14-s05 · Jules: My toes feel squeezed, and I have to climb steps on stage. Could I try the round-toed pair?
- p08-c-14-s06 · Rina: Of course. Those are flat and cost forty pounds.
- p08-c-14-s07 · Jules: They feel much better, and they're within my budget. I'll purchase this pair.

The ending selects them, matching the title and goal without claiming payment was processed; purchase is slightly formal but valid shop speech.

## 删去调查身份栏并澄清感受指标与分母 · p08-c-21

主题：研究与论证；情景：修订社区调查的隐私与结果表述（community-survey-privacy-revision）。
