- p08-c-21-s01 · Omar: Does every participant need to write a full name and address on this questionnaire?
- p08-c-21-s02 · Beth: No. We don't need those details, and collecting them would create a privacy concern.
- p08-c-21-s03 · Omar: I'll remove those boxes and state that participation is voluntary.
- p08-c-21-s04 · Beth: Good. What exactly are we asking about safety?
- p08-c-21-s05 · Omar: How people perceive the park after dark, not how many crimes actually happen there.
- p08-c-21-s06 · Beth: Then the report must call it a perception of safety. It can't present those answers as crime figures.
- p08-c-21-s07 · Omar: For the summary, I'll show the proportion who report feeling safe.
- p08-c-21-s08 · Beth: Say 'the proportion of people who answered that question'. Some may skip it.
- p08-c-21-s09 · Omar: Agreed. I'll correct the form and that heading before we share them.

## 请药师联系医生核实妊娠期间的处方问题 · p08-c-23

主题：健康与照护；情景：向药房说明妊娠与处方疑问（pharmacy-pregnancy-medicine-referral）。

沟通目标：顾客说明妊娠及药品疑问，药师安排联系处方医生核实并请顾客留下等待

- p08-c-23-s02 · Pharmacist: Yes, we can use the consultation room. What's your concern?
- p08-c-23-s03 · Naomi: I've just found out I'm pregnant. I don't know whether the physician who wrote this was aware.
- p08-c-23-s04 · Pharmacist: Thank you for telling me. I'll contact the prescriber to check the instructions.
- p08-c-23-s05 · Naomi: I brought the box because I wasn't sure whether this pill was the one mentioned in the letter.
- p08-c-23-s06 · Pharmacist: That will help us identify it. I won't guess from its appearance. Would you wait here while I make the call?
- p08-c-23-s07 · Naomi: Yes, I'll wait. Please let them know about the pregnancy.

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Naomi requests privacy, discloses pregnancy and uncertainty over a prescribed pill, and provides its box. The pharmacist offers to check with the prescriber instead of identifying medicine by appearance; Naomi agrees to wait and authorizes relaying the pregnancy. The ending matches a request/referral arrangement, not a completed clinical determination, and gives no drug or dose advice.

## 把拼图研究结论限定为本任务表现 · p08-c-30

主题：研究与论证；情景：审视拼图成绩的推论边界（puzzle-intelligence-claim-review）。

沟通目标：区分特定任务表现与整体智力，并依据练习影响修订课堂研究结论

背景：心理学课学生检查一项课堂拼图练习的结论。

人物：Tariq、Jo。

起草：author-phase08-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-c-30-s01 · Tariq: Our psychology report says the fastest person on the puzzle was the most intelligent. Is that justified?
- p08-c-30-s02 · Jo: No. Some people had already done that type of puzzle, and others had never seen one.
- p08-c-30-s03 · Tariq: So speed wasn't purely a measure of the ability we wanted to test.
- p08-c-30-s04 · Jo: Right. Practice could affect the result, and one task doesn't measure someone's overall intelligence.
- p08-c-30-s05 · Tariq: I'll change the conclusion to say who completed this task fastest under these conditions.
- p08-c-30-s06 · Jo: And note the difference in previous practice as a limitation.
- p08-c-30-s07 · Tariq: Done. The revised conclusion no longer ranks people's intelligence.

目标用法：

- psychology · n. · 心理学；义项 sense-staged-ox-d395a4a953af5d5f-p08-c-30；来源 ox-d395a4a953af5d5f；证据 p08-c-30-s01；词形 psychology。
- puzzle · n. · 拼图或益智题；义项 sense-staged-ox-ee7fc829d2ed3d91-p08-c-30；来源 ox-ee7fc829d2ed3d91；证据 p08-c-30-s01；词形 puzzle。
- purely · adv. · 完全地或仅仅；义项 sense-staged-ox-8863b881dec38d71-p08-c-30；来源 ox-8863b881dec38d71；证据 p08-c-30-s03；词形 purely。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The students reject ranking intelligence from puzzle speed because prior familiarity differs, then restrict their conclusion to performance on this task under these conditions. Tariq explicitly confirms the revision is done and Jo adds the practice limitation. The achieved narrowing matches the Chinese title/goal and avoids treating the classroom result as a valid intelligence measure.

- ox-d395a4a953af5d5f：accepted；In s01 psychology report identifies the academic subject of the class investigation. Psychology is a discipline noun used before report, matching 心理学 rather than someone’s individual mentality.（p08-c-30-s01）。
- ox-ee7fc829d2ed3d91：accepted；In s01 the puzzle is the timed problem participants completed; later references to its type and completion make the count noun 拼图或益智题 clear.（p08-c-30-s01）。
- ox-8863b881dec38d71：accepted；In s03 wasn’t purely a measure means speed did not reflect only the intended ability, because practice also influenced it. The limiting adverb fits 完全地或仅仅 under the negation.（p08-c-30-s03）。

## 更正紫外线展台告示并保留防护屏障 · p08-c-31

主题：学习与技能；情景：修订紫外线展台的操作与防护说明（uv-exhibit-safety-labels）。

沟通目标：依据展台安全说明删去观众可打开屏障的指示，改为由工作人员演示并保持防护

背景：学生布置由工作人员操作的科学展台，并核对现成的安全说明。

人物：Rose、Alex。

起草：author-phase08-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-c-31-s01 · Rose: My sign says visitors can open this cover to see the lamp more clearly.
- p08-c-31-s02 · Alex: That conflicts with the exhibit instructions. The cover provides protection from the radiation produced by the lamp.
- p08-c-31-s03 · Rose: Even when it doesn't look very bright?
- p08-c-31-s04 · Alex: Yes. The instructions describe direct exposure as potentially harmful. We shouldn't judge safety by brightness.
- p08-c-31-s05 · Rose: I'll replace the sign with 'Please ask a member of staff for a demonstration'.
- p08-c-31-s06 · Alex: Good. Keep the cover closed, and ask the supervisor to check the new sign before we open.

目标用法：

- protection · n. · 防护；义项 sense-staged-ox-0dfe1a0916792ec1-p08-c-31；来源 ox-0dfe1a0916792ec1；证据 p08-c-31-s02；词形 protection。
- radiation · n. · 辐射；义项 sense-staged-ox-4991a5a959716ee6-p08-c-31；来源 ox-4991a5a959716ee6；证据 p08-c-31-s02；词形 radiation。
- potentially · adv. · 潜在地或可能地；义项 sense-staged-ox-cefedd952f1a8469-p08-c-31；来源 ox-cefedd952f1a8469；证据 p08-c-31-s04；词形 potentially。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The students identify a direct conflict between a draft sign and the exhibit instructions, reject brightness as a safety test, and agree on staff demonstrations with the cover kept closed. Supervisor review remains required before opening. The Chinese title/goal accurately describe correction of the exhibit notice and retaining the protective barrier, not permission for visitors to operate the lamp.

- ox-4991a5a959716ee6：accepted；In s02 radiation produced by the lamp refers to emitted energy in this UV-exhibit setting; the uncountable noun 辐射 is tied to the exhibit’s safety instructions rather than an invented dose claim.（p08-c-31-s02）。

- p08-c-32-s04 · Anton: It pumps blood. The lungs take oxygen from the air into the blood.
- p08-c-32-s05 · Ruby: I'll swap the model labels and change the diagram to show the heart pumping blood.

## 把哲学读书讨论聚焦于分配机会的公平原则 · p08-c-33

主题：学习与技能；情景：确定哲学读书组的具体讨论问题（philosophy-reading-group-focus）。

沟通目标：将宽泛的求知主题缩小为公平分配活动名额的原则，并约定携带实例参与讨论

背景：新成立的读书组在选择第一次哲学讨论的切入点。

人物：Oona、Felix。

起草：author-phase08-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-c-33-s01 · Oona: I joined because I'm interested in philosophy, but 'the pursuit of truth' feels too broad for one evening.
- p08-c-33-s02 · Felix: What about a practical question: how should a club divide limited places on a trip?
- p08-c-33-s03 · Oona: We could examine the principle that everyone should have an equal chance.
- p08-c-33-s04 · Felix: And ask whether drawing names is fairer than giving places to whoever pays first.
- p08-c-33-s05 · Oona: That's specific enough. I'll bring the example from our walking club.
- p08-c-33-s06 · Felix: I'll find a short reading on fairness. Let's use that question for our first meeting.

- philosophy · n. · 哲学；义项 sense-staged-ox-490e940a619b5a07-p08-c-33；来源 ox-490e940a619b5a07；证据 p08-c-33-s01；词形 philosophy。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The readers turn an unmanageably broad truth-seeking theme into a concrete fairness question about limited trip places, comparing a lottery with first payment. Oona offers a walking-club example and Felix a short reading, so the first discussion’s focus and preparation are agreed exactly as the Chinese goal states.

## 删除未经证实的纯水说明并保留冻结观察 · p08-c-35

主题：学习与技能；情景：限定冻结实验记录中的材料断言（freezing-demo-observation-limits）。

沟通目标：发现未核实水的纯度，决定记录实际容器标签和冻结现象而不声称是纯水实验

背景：学生查看老师提供的冻结演示记录。

人物：Hugo、Seren。

起草：author-phase08-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-c-35-s01 · Hugo: I've described this as a test using pure water. Do we know that's what was in the bottle?
- p08-c-35-s02 · Seren: The label just says tap water. We didn't measure what else was in it.
- p08-c-35-s03 · Hugo: Then I can't use the result to make a precise claim about pure water.
- p08-c-35-s04 · Seren: No. We can describe the phenomenon we observed: ice formed first around the edge.
- p08-c-35-s05 · Hugo: I'll keep that observation and replace the material description with what the label actually says.
- p08-c-35-s06 · Seren: And add that we didn't check the contents independently. That makes the limit clear.

目标用法：

- pure · adj. · 纯净的；义项 sense-staged-ox-c2eda3b6c792baf0-p08-c-35；来源 ox-c2eda3b6c792baf0；证据 p08-c-35-s01；词形 pure。

审阅者：reviewer-phase08-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The students notice that a tap-water label and no independent measurement cannot justify calling the sample pure. They agree to retain only the observed edge-first freezing and actual label description, with an explicit verification limitation. This matches the Chinese correction goal and does not generalize their classroom observation into a universal freezing law.

## 约定私下了解种族偏见报告的处理流程 · p08-c-38

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：约定私下了解种族偏见报告流程（club-racism-report-private-meeting）。

沟通目标：说明种族偏见言论及隐私顾虑，约定私下了解处理流程与信息使用方式后再决定分享内容

背景：社团成员向负责人报告上次活动中发生的不当言论。

人物：Amina、Gareth。

起草：author-phase08-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p08-c-38-s01 · Amina: Someone made a racist remark about my family at the last meeting. I don't want to repeat it in front of the whole group.
- p08-c-38-s02 · Gareth: You don't have to. We can talk privately and record what happened in your own words.
- p08-c-38-s03 · Amina: They said I shouldn't be so sensitive about my racial background.
- p08-c-38-s04 · Gareth: That doesn't make the comment acceptable. Our rules against racism apply to how members treat each other, not just to public posts.
- p08-c-38-s05 · Amina: I'm willing to explain what happened, but I want to know who will see the report.
- p08-c-38-s06 · Gareth: Let's meet in the office after this session. I'll explain the process and how the information would be used before you decide what to share.
- p08-c-38-s07 · Amina: All right. I'll come then, and we can start with that explanation.
